9.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/rdp_gaussian_single.json 1 Gaussian, sigma=1.0, l2=1.0, alpha=2 rdp=0.500
fixtures/rdp_gaussian_100compose.json 100 Gaussian, sigma=1.0, delta=1e-5 eps in [1.0, 2.0]
fixtures/rdp_laplace_alpha3.json 1 Laplace, l1=1.0, b=1.0, alpha=3 verify formula numerically
fixtures/rdp_rr_eps1.json RR with eps=1.0 cross-check Mironov Appendix
fixtures/rdp_to_eps_delta_sweep.json vary delta 1e-3 to 1e-10 eps monotonically increases as delta decreases
fixtures/rdp_invalid_alpha.json alpha=1.0 Err(InvalidAlpha)
fixtures/rdp_mironov_table1.json Table 1 entries from Mironov 2017 all within 1e-6

9.6 Pitfalls

  • Off-by-one in the conversion formula: Mironov 2017 Prop 3 uses (alpha-1) in the denominator; mistyping as alpha shifts the bound. Signal: rdp_gaussian_single fixture fails.
  • Numerical instability at large alpha: log(delta * (alpha_i-1)) for alpha=1024, delta=1e-10 produces very large negative values; subtraction with rdp[i] may cancel. Use log1p and split the expression.
  • Wrong order grid resolution: too-sparse grid (e.g. alpha=[2, 1024]) misses the optimal alpha, returns eps 20-50% larger. Fix: use the 14-point default grid; expose override for advanced users.
  • Laplace RDP formula overflow: exp((alpha-1)*l1/b) for alpha=1024, l1=1, b=0.01 = exp(102300) → inf. Use log-sum-exp: compute in log space, exponentiate once.
  • Composition with heterogeneous order grids: two mechanisms computed on different alpha grids cannot be composed element-wise. Fix: require a single global order grid at RdpAccountant::new; reject mismatched rdp_curve lengths with Err(OrderMismatch).
  • Forgetting to include delta_prime in budget when converting: the (eps, delta) returned by to_eps_delta uses the supplied delta as the conversion target, which must be drawn from the privacy budget; operator must ensure it matches the budget delta ceiling.

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • compose_mechanism: element-wise addition over a 14-element array — O(14) scalar adds, SIMD-friendly; no allocation.
  • to_eps_delta: O(k) loop over orders with one log + division per order; < 1 µs for k=14.
  • rdp_for_gaussian / rdp_for_laplace: O(1) per order; batch via rdp_curve(...) for full grid in one call.
  • Storing rdp: Vec<f64> (14 f64 = 112 bytes) per accountant: negligible; no cache pressure.
  • Hot path for DP-SGD (Phase 10/13): thousands of compose_mechanism calls per training step; keep inner loop branchless and allocation-free.

9.8 Edge Cases

  • orders vector length = 1: valid; single-order accountant; conversion formula still applies.
  • rdp values all zero before first compose: to_eps_delta returns eps = conversion of (0, delta) = 0.0; valid.
  • Delta = 1e-300 (extremely small): log(delta) = -690; formula must not underflow to -inf before subtraction.
  • Alpha approaching 1 from above (e.g. 1.0001): formula numerically sensitive near the limit; minimum allowed alpha is 1.01 in the default grid. to_eps_delta when all rdp values are infinity: return Err(InfiniteRdp).
  • Subsampling amplification: RDP under subsampling uses a different (more complex) formula; do not apply the Gaussian formula directly for subsampled mechanisms — that is Phase 10's concern; rdp_curve returns Err(UseSubsampledFormula) for those.

9.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Formula regression (code change breaks Gaussian RDP) Fixture rdp_gaussian_single.json deviates All Gaussian-mechanism accounting wrong Revert; pin formula with doc-comment reference to paper equation number
Order grid too sparse — suboptimal eps Statistical validation in Phase 14 shows large slack Wasted utility (over-noise); no privacy violation Rebuild accountant with denser grid; re-run queries
RDP conversion returns eps < true composed eps (bug) Phase 14 neighboring-dataset test exceeds e^eps All Gaussian releases violate claimed guarantee Emergency: fall back to advanced composition (Phase 8) until fixed
Accountant state loss on process crash Budget manager restart (Phase 11) finds no rdp state Budget treated as unused — can over-allocate Phase 11 persists rdp vec + orders to durable storage

9.10 Operational Notes

  • Metric: dp_rdp_total{order_index="0..13"} gauge per order per accountant instance.
  • Metric: dp_rdp_optimal_alpha gauge: the order index that minimized eps at last to_eps_delta call; useful for diagnosing grid quality.
  • Log at DEBUG: full rdp curve on each compose (gated behind rdp_verbose); at INFO: only the resulting (eps, delta) at each budget-check call.
  • Conversion method (Mironov2017 vs BalleEtAl2020) selected at accountant construction; recorded in audit log; changing mid-session is prohibited (invalidates prior accounting).
  • Phase 10 extends this struct in-place (adds subsampled-Gaussian and zCDP paths); no breaking API change; AccountantKind::Rdp remains stable.

Phase 10 — zCDP + moments accountant

10.1 Objectives

  • Implement zero-concentrated DP (ZcdpAccountant) in dp-accountant: track rho accumulator, additive composition, conversion to (eps,delta).
  • Implement the moments accountant as an RDP specialization covering the subsampled Gaussian for DP-SGD.
  • Implement subsampling amplification: Poisson subsampling ratio q reduces per-step RDP cost.
  • Expose convert_zcdp_to_approx_dp(rho, delta) -> Epsilon and convert_rdp_to_approx_dp(orders, rdp, delta) -> Epsilon.
  • All arithmetic is numerically stable; no overflow or NaN escapes into a PrivacyParams.

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

dp-accountant/src/zcdp.rs

  • ZcdpAccountant { rho: f64 } — rho >= 0.0; constructed via ZcdpAccountant::new() (rho=0).
  • fn charge_gaussian(&mut self, l2: f64, sigma: f64) — adds l2.powi(2) / (2.0 * sigma.powi(2)); validates sigma > 0.
  • fn compose(&mut self, other: ZcdpAccountant) — self.rho += other.rho; panics on negative rho post-add (I9).
  • fn to_approx_dp(&self, delta: Delta) -> Result<Epsilon, DpError> — formula: eps = rho + 2*(rho * ln(1/delta)).sqrt(); reject delta=0 (delta=0 would require a pure-DP proof unavailable here).
  • fn to_rdp_curve(&self, orders: &[f64]) -> Vec<f64> — converts rho to RDP: rdp[i] = rho * orders[i]; composable with RdpAccountant.

dp-accountant/src/moments.rs

  • MomentsAccountant { orders: Vec<f64>, log_moments: Vec<f64> } — stores log moment generating function.
  • fn charge_subsampled_gaussian(&mut self, l2: f64, sigma: f64, q: f64, steps: u64) — per Abadi et al. CCS 2016 algorithm; iterates orders; for each alpha computes the log moment bound for the subsampled Gaussian mechanism (using the bounding expression over the three terms) multiplied by steps; adds to log_moments.
  • fn get_privacy_spent(&self, delta: Delta) -> Result<Epsilon, DpError> — for each order alpha: eps_alpha = (log_moment - delta.0.ln()) / (alpha - 1.0); returns min over alpha.
  • Numerical care: compute in log-space; clamp alpha >= 1.0 + f64::EPSILON; handle log_moment = f64::NEG_INFINITY (no cost added yet).

dp-accountant/src/rdp.rs (extensions from Phase 9)

  • Add fn from_zcdp(zcdp: &ZcdpAccountant, orders: &[f64]) -> RdpAccountant bridge.
  • Add fn subsampling_amplify(&mut self, q: f64) — applies Poisson-subsampling amplification lemma per order (Mironov 2017 Theorem 3); replaces each rdp[i] with the amplified bound.

Integration in dp-accountant/src/lib.rs

  • Re-export ZcdpAccountant, MomentsAccountant; expose unified AccountantKind enum: { Basic, Advanced, Rdp(RdpAccountant), Zcdp(ZcdpAccountant), Moments(MomentsAccountant) }.
  • fn compose_all(accountants: &[AccountantKind], delta: Delta) -> Result<PrivacyLoss, DpError> — convert each to (eps,delta) at requested delta, take the tightest.

Tests (dp-accountant/tests/zcdp_moments.rs)

  • Table-driven: sigma=1.0 with q=0.01, steps=1000 → expected eps at delta=1e-5 cross-checked against published Table 1 of Abadi et al.
  • zCDP round-trip: ZcdpAccountant → to_approx_dp vs RdpAccountant.to_approx_dp within 1e-9.
  • Monotonicity (I9): adding a charge never decreases epsilon at any delta.

10.3 Deliverable

  • ZcdpAccountant and MomentsAccountant compile and pass all unit tests.
  • cargo test -p dp-accountant green.
  • dp-dpsgd (Phase 13) can call MomentsAccountant::charge_subsampled_gaussian to get a running epsilon.
  • CLI dp-cli accountant --type moments prints epsilon at user-supplied delta.

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
zCDP → (eps,delta) for rho=0.5, delta=1e-6 matches formula within 1e-12
Moments accountant, sigma=1.1, q=0.001, steps=10000, delta=1e-5 eps <= 2.0 (published bound)
RDP-from-zCDP vs zCDP direct conversion absolute diff < 1e-9
compose monotonicity over 1000 random charges zero regressions
to_approx_dp(delta=0) returns Err(DpError::DeltaMustBePositive)

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected output
fixtures/zcdp_basic.json rho=0.25, delta=1e-5 eps value to 10 sig figs
fixtures/moments_table1.json Abadi Table 1 params eps within 0.01 of published
fixtures/subsampling_amplify.json q=0.1, sigma=2.0, alpha=3..20 per-order amplified RDP values
fixtures/malformed_sigma_zero.json sigma=0.0 Err(DpError::InvalidSigma)

10.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal
Overflow in log-moment sum eps = NaN Large alpha * steps without clamping Add log_moment.is_finite() assertion post-charge
Subsampling amplification ignored eps reported too large → too much noise Forgot to call subsampling_amplify before conversion Compare moment vs basic composition; large discrepancy flags it
zCDP delta=0 misuse to_approx_dp returns nonsense sqrt of ln(1/0) = inf Validate delta > 0 at API boundary; test with delta=0
Off-by-one in steps multiplier Epsilon 2x too large/small steps should multiply log-moment, not sigma Cross-check single-step vs 1-step moments
alpha=1 division-by-zero in RDP conversion NaN in min search (alpha-1) denominator Skip or handle alpha=1 via L'Hopital limit

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • charge_subsampled_gaussian: O(|orders|) inner loop; for |orders|=256 and steps=10000 the total ops = 2.56M FP; run once at epoch boundary, not per-sample.
  • to_approx_dp: O(|orders|) min-scan; negligible.
  • Avoid heap allocation inside the log-moment loop; pre-allocate log_moments at MomentsAccountant::new(orders).
  • Log-space summation avoids catastrophic cancellation; never compute exp(large) - exp(large).

10.8 Edge Cases

  • steps=0 → epsilon=0 (no queries issued); return PrivacyParams { epsilon: Epsilon(0.0 + EPSILON), delta } — or define 0-step as pure-zero loss.
  • q=1.0 (full-batch, no amplification) → amplified bound equals unamplified; verify no underflow.
  • q=0.0 → privacy loss = 0 (no samples drawn); guard division in amplification formula.
  • orders list length 0 → Err(DpError::EmptyOrders).
  • sigma very large (e.g. 1e10) → rho underflows to 0; treat as zero loss gracefully.

10.9 Failure Analysis

  • Incorrect moments bound (moments table diverges from reference): blast radius = all DP-SGD eps claims wrong; detection = Phase 14 cross-check vs advanced composition upper bound; recovery = audit charge_subsampled_gaussian against Algorithm 3 in Abadi et al.
  • NaN propagation from bad alpha: budget sees NaN < ceiling → passes incorrectly; detection = PrivacyLoss::is_valid() check before every budget charge; containment = reject NaN/Inf at charge boundary (I10).
  • Monotonicity violation (I9): accountant reports lower eps after adding a mechanism; detection = assert new_eps >= prev_eps in test; recovery = likely sign error in log-moment accumulation.

10.10 Operational Notes

  • Log every charge_subsampled_gaussian call: sigma, q, steps, resulting rho/eps (never raw data).
  • Expose ZcdpAccountant::rho() and MomentsAccountant::current_epsilon(delta) as metrics for budget dashboard.
  • Tracing span: dp_accountant::zcdp::charge, dp_accountant::moments::charge.
  • Migration: if upgrading from basic composition (Phase 8), rerun historical charges through MomentsAccountant to derive tighter reported epsilon; do not revoke prior releases.

Phase 11 — privacy budget manager

11.1 Objectives

  • Implement dp-budget crate: Budget struct with per-dataset and per-analyst ledgers, hard (eps,delta) ceiling.
  • Every release charges the accountant, then atomically updates the ledger; a query that would exceed remaining budget is denied with Err(DpError::BudgetExhausted) — nothing is emitted (I3, I10).
  • Audit log records every charge: timestamp, query id, mechanism, eps, delta, remaining budget.
  • Budget cannot go negative; spent field is monotonically non-decreasing.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

dp-budget/src/ledger.rs

struct BudgetEntry {
    query_id:   Uuid,
    mechanism:  String,   // "Laplace", "Gaussian", etc.
    charged:    PrivacyParams,
    remaining:  PrivacyParams,
    timestamp:  u64,      // Unix ms
}

BudgetLedger { entries: Vec<BudgetEntry> } — append-only; serializable to JSON/CBOR. record(&mut self, entry) — O(1) push; never modifies existing entries. total_spent(&self) -> PrivacyLoss — reduce via accountant composition over all charged fields.

dp-budget/src/budget.rs

struct Budget {
    id:       Uuid,           // dataset or analyst id
    total:    PrivacyParams,  // hard ceiling
    spent:    PrivacyLoss,    // current accumulation
    ledger:   BudgetLedger,
    accountant: AccountantKind,
}

new(id, total, accountant_kind) validates total.epsilon > 0. try_charge(&mut self, cost, query_id, mechanism) -> Result<PrivacyLoss, DpError>: (1) compose spent with cost via accountant → candidate_loss; (2) convert to (eps,delta) at total.delta; (3) if candidate_eps > total.epsilon OR candidate_delta > total.delta → Err(BudgetExhausted) (emit nothing); (4) atomically set spent = candidate_loss, record in ledger, return Ok. remaining(&self) — total - spent as tightest (eps,delta). Atomicity: try_charge is &mut self; concurrent use needs Arc<Mutex<Budget>> (SharedBudget alias). snapshot(&self) -> BudgetSnapshot — serializable view of total/spent/remaining for observability without exposing raw ledger.

dp-budget/src/manager.rs: BudgetManager { budgets: HashMap<Uuid, SharedBudget> } — multi-dataset registry. register(id, total, accountant_kind) idempotent; errors if id registered with a different ceiling. charge(id, cost, query_id, mechanism) delegates to Budget::try_charge. audit_log(id) -> Vec<BudgetEntry> — read-only ledger snapshot.

dp-budget/src/errors.rs: DpError::BudgetExhausted { dataset_id, requested, remaining } carries diagnostics; DatasetNotFound, DuplicateDataset.

Tests (dp-budget/tests/budget.rs): sequential charges exhaust budget on the N+1-th call; concurrent charges under Arc<Mutex<Budget>> (10 threads, ceil(total/9) each) — exactly 9 succeed; budget never goes negative (proptest); audit log length equals successful-charge count.

Anchors: P4, P11, I3, I11.

11.3 Deliverable

  • cargo test -p dp-budget green.
  • BudgetManager usable from dp-query (Phase 12) and dp-dpsgd (Phase 13).
  • dp-cli budget show <id> prints total/spent/remaining and last N ledger entries.
  • dp-cli budget audit <id> dumps full ledger as JSON.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
Sequential N exhaustion test passes with 100 different (eps,delta) ceilings
Concurrent 10-thread test no charge accepted after exhaustion; no panics
Budget never negative 10000 proptest cases
try_charge latency (single-threaded) < 1 µs per call (no I/O path)
Audit log serialization round-trip byte-identical for 1000-entry logs

11.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected
fixtures/exhaustion_sequence.json 10 charges summing to exactly eps ceiling 10th charge OK; 11th → BudgetExhausted
fixtures/rdp_budget_integration.json RDP accountant, 5 Gaussian queries spent eps at delta=1e-5 matches RDP conversion
fixtures/malformed_ceiling.json total epsilon=0 Err(DpError::InvalidEpsilon)
fixtures/duplicate_register.json register same id twice second call with same ceiling: no-op; different ceiling: error
fixtures/audit_roundtrip.cbor 100-entry ledger CBOR deserialize → same entries

11.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug / Recovery
TOCTOU in multi-thread path two threads both pass the check before either commits Lock not held across check-and-commit Use Mutex span covering the full try_charge body; never split lock
Accountant rounding allows fractional over-spend eps slightly > ceiling passes f64 comparison with no epsilon tolerance Compare with strict <=; add tiny rho tolerance only if proven safe
Ledger grows unbounded in long sessions OOM in multi-day runs Append-only with no rotation Implement trim_ledger(max_entries) that archives to disk, keeps last N in memory
Delta budget ignored unlimited releases with small eps Only checking epsilon arm of budget Check BOTH eps and delta arms in step 3 of try_charge
Spent not monotonic after deserialization budget appears to reset Snapshot loaded without replaying ledger Always recompute spent from ledger on load, not from snapshot field

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • try_charge hot path: one accountant composition + one conversion to (eps,delta) + one HashMap lookup + one Vec push.
  • Lock contention: Arc<Mutex<Budget>> is per-dataset; per-analyst budgets are independent locks.
  • Ledger serialization (audit dump): batch serialize; do not hold mutex during disk write — copy entries, then release.
  • HashMap<Uuid,SharedBudget>: pre-size if dataset count is known; UUID hashing is constant-time.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • try_charge with cost.epsilon = 0.0 → reject (Epsilon must be > 0, I8); an epsilon-0 charge is meaningless.
  • remaining exactly equals cost → charge must succeed (boundary: <= not <).
  • Budget with delta = 0 (pure DP ceiling): deny any mechanism with delta > 0; only pure-DP mechanisms are admitted.
  • BudgetManager with 0 registered datasets: all charge calls return Err(DatasetNotFound).
  • Deserialization of a ledger where computed spent exceeds total (tampered log): return Err(LedgerCorrupted).

11.9 Failure Analysis

  • Silent over-spend (I3 violation): query served despite budget exhaustion; highest severity; detection = Phase 14 audit crosses charges against ceiling; containment = lock discipline + boundary check correctness.
  • Deadlock under nested budget charges: two datasets cross-charging each other; detection = lock-order analysis; recovery = enforce lock acquisition in Uuid-sorted order across BudgetManager.
  • Ledger corruption on crash mid-write: partially written entry; detection = checksum per entry; recovery = replay from last valid entry, recompute spent.
  • Clock skew in timestamps: audit log ordering unreliable; use monotonic clock for ordering, wall clock for human display only.

11.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: dp_budget_remaining_epsilon{dataset_id}, dp_budget_charges_total{dataset_id}, dp_budget_exhausted_total{dataset_id}.
  • Tracing span: dp_budget::try_charge; include query_id in span fields.
  • Log every BudgetExhausted at WARN level with remaining/requested; never log raw data or un-noised values (P10).
  • Deployment: budget state is in-process; for durability across restarts, serialize ledger to disk atomically (write-rename pattern) at configurable intervals.
  • Rollback: if a downstream mechanism fails after charge was committed, budget is NOT reversed (fail-closed: over-counting is safe; under-counting is not).

Phase 12 — DP query interface

12.1 Objectives

  • Implement dp-query crate: Query trait with five concrete query types — count, bounded sum, mean, histogram, quantile.
  • Each query computes its sensitivity, selects a mechanism, charges the budget, and returns DpResult<T>.
  • Parallel composition for histogram (disjoint bins compose in parallel — total eps = max bin eps).
  • Quantile via exponential mechanism (candidates from sorted array, utility = negative abs distance).
  • Mean: two strategies — (a) single charged release of sum/count with combined sensitivity, or (b) two separate releases (sum + count) each charged independently.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

dp-query/src/traits.rs

trait Query {
    type Output;
    fn sensitivity(&self, bounds: &Bounds) -> Result<Sensitivity, DpError>;
    fn exact(&self, data: &[f64]) -> Self::Output;
    fn run(&self, data: &[f64], bounds: &Bounds,
           params: &PrivacyParams, budget: &mut Budget) -> Result<DpResult<Self::Output>, DpError>;
}

run calls sensitivity → constructs mechanism → budget.try_charge → mechanism.privatize → wraps in DpResult. If try_charge fails, run returns Err; no noise added, nothing emitted.

dp-query/src/count.rs: CountQuery; sensitivity = { l1:1.0, l2:1.0 } (add/remove neighbor); uses LaplaceMechanism for pure-DP (delta=0) or GaussianMechanism when delta>0; exact = data.len() as f64.

dp-query/src/bounded_sum.rs: BoundedSumQuery { bounds }; sensitivity.l1 = bounds.upper - bounds.lower = sensitivity.l2; clamps each element to [lower,upper] before summing (I6); uses Laplace or Gaussian based on params.delta.

dp-query/src/mean.rs: Strategy A (MeanQuerySingle) treats as clamped sum/n; sensitivity (upper-lower)/n, n must be fixed (public) or sensitivity is data-dependent. Strategy B (MeanQueryTwo) releases sum and count as separate queries, charges budget twice, combines post-release (post-processing, no extra cost). Default Strategy B; Strategy A available when n_public: usize is declared.

dp-query/src/histogram.rs: HistogramQuery { bins: Vec<(f64,f64)> } — non-overlapping contiguous intervals. Per-bin sensitivity = 1 (count); parallel composition: total DP = single-bin DP (not sum). exact classifies each value into a bin (values outside all bins dropped or into underflow/overflow bins). run iterates bins as CountQuery::run, charges budget once using parallel composition (max per-bin charge, not sum); returns DpResult<Vec<f64>>.

dp-query/src/quantile.rs: QuantileQuery { quantile, candidates } — candidates sorted, analyst-provided, data-independent. Uses ExponentialMechanism; utility for candidate c = -|rank_fraction(c) - quantile| (rank_fraction computed on data clamped to [candidates.min, candidates.max]); sensitivity = 1.0/candidates.len(); charges budget via ExponentialMechanism::advertised_params.

dp-query/src/lib.rs: re-exports all five; QueryKind enum for dp-cli; fn run_query(kind, data, bounds, params, budget) -> Result<DpResult<Box<dyn Any>>, DpError>.

Tests (dp-query/tests/): count_laplace.rs — noised count within 3*scale of true count (seed-fixed); bounded_sum_sensitivity.rs — declared >= true L1 sensitivity; histogram_parallel.rs — k-bin charge = 1-bin charge; quantile_exponential.rs — output always in candidates, biased toward true quantile over 1000 trials; mean_two_strategy.rs — two-charge mean charges twice, single-charge with public n charges once.

12.3 Deliverable

  • cargo test -p dp-query green.
  • dp-cli query count --data file.csv --epsilon 1.0 --bounds 0,100 executes end-to-end through sensitivity → mechanism → budget → DpResult.
  • All five query types wire through BudgetManager.
  • Returned DpResult<T> carries the actual PrivacyParams used (after mechanism selection).

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
histogram_parallel budget test 10-bin histogram costs 1 bin's eps, not 10x
bounded_sum sensitivity >= true holds for 10000 random Bounds
quantile output membership always in candidates (100% of trials)
mean_two_strategy charges budget twice verified by Budget::ledger.len() == 2
run on exhausted budget returns Err(BudgetExhausted), no value emitted
E2E CLI count query exit code 0, JSON output with value and params fields

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected
fixtures/count_pure_dp.json 100 records, eps=1.0, delta=0 Laplace noise, sensitivity=1
fixtures/bounded_sum_clamping.json values outside bounds clamped before sum; sensitivity = upper-lower
fixtures/histogram_10bins.json uniform data, 10 equal bins eps charged = single-bin eps
fixtures/quantile_median.json sorted candidates, true median in candidates output converges to median over 1000 seeds
fixtures/mean_public_n.json n=1000 declared, single charge budget.ledger.len() == 1 after run
fixtures/malformed_bounds_inverted.json lower > upper Err(DpError::InvalidBounds)

12.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug / Recovery
Histogram using sequential composition eps billed k times Forgot parallel composition rule Assert ledger length = 1 after k-bin histogram
Mean sensitivity ignores n (data-dependent) sensitivity < true → privacy failure Strategy A used without fixed public n Enforce n_public is declared or reject Strategy A
Quantile candidate set not data-independent exponential mechanism privacy broken Candidates derived from data API doc + runtime assert: candidates must be fixed before seeing data
Clamping after noise sensitivity undefined Clip applied post-mechanism Enforce clamp-then-aggregate in exact computation
Budget charged before noise fails eps spent but no result Mechanism panics after charge Charge only on success path; or implement try_reserve + commit/rollback pattern

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • HistogramQuery with large k: k calls to CountQuery::exact (each O(n)); refactor to single O(n*k) pass over data.
  • QuantileQuery with large candidates and n: rank_fraction is O(n) per candidate → O(n*|candidates|); precompute sorted data + binary search → O(n log n + |candidates| log n).
  • BoundedSumQuery clamp: single O(n) pass; no allocation if data is borrowed.
  • Mechanism object construction per query: stack-allocated structs; no heap overhead.

12.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty data (data.len() == 0): count=0, sum=0, histogram all zeros — all valid; noise still added.
  • All values outside histogram bins: all bins zero; out-of-bounds handling must not panic.
  • quantile=0.0 or 1.0: min/max; exponential mechanism biases toward endpoints; valid.
  • Bounds { lower, upper } where lower == upper: sensitivity=0 → noise scale=0 for Laplace; eps=0 is invalid (I8) → treat as degenerate or pure_dp_with_eps=EPSILON.
  • mean_two_strategy if budget has only enough for one charge: first charge succeeds, second fails → mean not released; first charge (count) already consumed — document this asymmetry; consider transactional reservation.

12.9 Failure Analysis

  • Sensitivity under-estimation (I2 violation): noise too small → privacy broken; blast radius = all queries of that type; detection = Phase 14 sensitivity audit; recovery = patch sensitivity formula (prior releases cannot be retracted).
  • Parallel composition misapplied (histogram billed sequentially): wastes budget; detection = budget-usage monitoring shows k× expected spend; recovery = reissue queries with corrected accounting.
  • Quantile candidates data-dependent: exponential mechanism proof invalid; detection = code-review/static API analysis; recovery = require candidates declared before data access at API level.
  • Budget charge committed but output lost (e.g. serialization error): eps spent, no result delivered; detection = ledger entry without corresponding DpResult; recovery = caller treats missing result as budgeted loss, does not re-query without new charge.

12.10 Operational Notes

  • Log every run call: query type, epsilon, delta, sensitivity, mechanism chosen, dataset id (never raw data or exact answer).
  • Metric: dp_query_runs_total{type, mechanism}, dp_query_budget_exhausted_total{type}.
  • Tracing span: dp_query::run; child span dp_budget::try_charge.
  • DpResult<T> serialized to JSON includes params.epsilon, params.delta, mechanism, sensitivity_used — a full privacy certificate for the release.
  • CLI dp-cli query accepts --format json|text; JSON mode emits the full DpResult envelope; text mode emits value only.
  • Cross-crate dependency order: dp-query depends on dp-sensitivity, dp-mechanism, dp-budget, dp-core; does not depend on dp-sample directly (that is dp-mechanism's concern).

Phase 13 — local DP + DP-SGD + CLI

13.1 Objectives

  • Implement dp-local: randomized response (binary + k-ary), unbiased frequency estimator, heavy-hitter sketch.
  • Implement dp-dpsgd: per-example gradient clipping (L2 bound C), batch Gaussian noise injection, moments-accountant epsilon tracking across training steps.
  • Implement dp-cli: subcommands query, budget, mechanism, validate; JSON I/O; file-backed budget ledger.
  • Wire all crates under a single CLI entry-point; no implicit re-noising of derived outputs (P5, I7).
  • Budget charges for local-DP and DP-SGD flows routed through dp-budget (I3).

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

dp-local

Item Detail
RandomizedResponse struct { p: f64 } where p = e^eps/(e^eps+1); privatize(bit) -> bool; eps-DP (pure)
k-ary RR KaryRR { k: usize, p: f64 } where p = (e^eps)/(e^eps + k - 1); uniform off-outcome selection
Debiasing estimator debias(noisy_freq: f64, k: usize, p: f64) -> f64; formula (f - 1/k) / (p - 1/k)
Hadamard heavy-hitter HadamardSketch { bits: usize, rng } — each user hashes domain to {-1,+1}^b, applies RR on each bit, server sums and recovers top-k via inverse Hadamard; sensitivity 1 per user
Frequency oracle Aggregate per-user RR into server-side estimate; outputs Vec<(value, estimated_freq)> sorted descending
Budget integration Each collection round charges (eps_rr, 0) via Budget::spend; parallel composition across disjoint bins

dp-dpsgd

Item Detail
ClipGradient fn Clips per-example gradient tensor (flat Vec<f64>) to L2 norm <= C; scale factor `min(1, C/
add_gaussian_noise fn Adds N(0, sigma^2) to each coordinate of the summed gradient; sigma = C * gaussian_scale
DpSgd struct { clip_norm: f64, noise_multiplier: f64, batch_size: usize, accountant: RdpAccountant }
step fn Signature fn step(&mut self, per_example_grads: &[Vec<f64>]) -> (Vec<f64>, PrivacyParams)
Moments accountant Each step charges dp-accountant via subsampled Gaussian RDP formula: q = batch_size/N; order vector reused from Phase 9/10
Subsampling amplification Applied only through RdpAccountant::charge_subsampled_gaussian; never assumed ad-hoc (P9)
Training loop hook DpSgdTrainer::train loops epochs, calls step, calls Budget::spend per epoch, raises BudgetExhausted when ceiling hit

dp-cli

Subcommand Flags Output
query --query count|sum|mean|hist|quantile --file data.csv --eps EPS --delta DELTA --bounds L,U JSON DpResult<T>
budget --init --eps EPS --delta DELTA / --status / --spend EPS DELTA JSON ledger snapshot
mechanism --mech laplace|gaussian|exp|rrmax --eps EPS --delta DELTA --sens SENS JSON noisied value + params
validate --mechanism MECH --eps EPS --trials N JSON pass/fail + p-value
File-backed ledger budget.json via serde_json; atomic write via temp-file + rename; path from --budget-file flag
Structured logging tracing JSON subscriber; --log-level flag; no raw data or exact query answers logged (P10, I10)

13.3 Deliverable

  • dp-local crate: RandomizedResponse, KaryRR, HadamardSketch, frequency oracle with debiasing.
  • dp-dpsgd crate: DpSgd::step, moments-accountant wiring, BudgetExhausted error propagation.
  • dp-cli binary: all four subcommands exercised; round-trip CSV -> DpResult JSON.
  • Integration test: CLI query count charges and reduces a file-backed budget ledger.

Check Threshold

13.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Detection
Off-by-one in k-ary RR Frequency estimates systematically biased k counted as k+1 in debiasing formula Fixture kary_rr_k8_eps2.json mean check
Subsampling rate wrong DpSGD epsilon severely underestimated Using batch_size instead of batch_size/dataset_size as q Compare against reference compute_dp_sgd_privacy
Gradient accumulation before clip Privacy broken: sensitivity exceeds C Summing gradients then clipping rather than clip-then-sum Sensitivity audit in dp-validate
Budget double-charge Remaining budget halved unexpectedly Calling Budget::spend twice per query (once in query layer, once in CLI) Budget ledger diff test
Non-atomic ledger write Corrupt budget.json on crash Write in place without temp-file rename Simulate kill during write; parse ledger on restart
Heavy-hitter domain mismatch Zero recall Server domain not matching client domain encoding Fixture replay with known domain

13.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Mitigation
Per-example gradient clipping (DpSGD) Vectorize L2 norm with SIMD; avoid alloc per example
Heavy-hitter Hadamard transform Use in-place Walsh-Hadamard; O(n log n) per round
File-backed budget ledger Atomic write via rename; keep ledger small (JSON lines)
CLI startup overhead Lazy crate init; avoid loading unused mechanism modules
Moments accountant per step Pre-compute RDP table at training init; incremental addition is O(orders)

13.8 Edge Cases

Case Handling
eps = 0 in RR Reject at Epsilon newtype construction (I8)
k = 1 in k-ary RR Degenerate: no privacy, debiasing undefined; return InvalidParams
Empty gradient slice in DpSgd Return zero gradient + charge nothing; log warn
Budget file missing at query time Create new ledger with zero-spend state; warn operator
CSV with non-numeric values Parse error propagated; no partial release
clip_norm = 0 Return zero gradient; do not divide by zero
Batch size > dataset size Clamp sampling rate q to 1.0; RDP formula still applies

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Recovery
DpSgd underestimates epsilon Moments accountant returns eps < truth Privacy violation for all training runs using this instance Audit against reference; retrain with conservative sigma
Budget ledger corruption JSON parse error on startup All queries rejected until ledger repaired Restore from backup; reconstruct from audit log
k-ary RR debiasing returns >1 freq Assertion in freq-oracle output check Misleading heavy-hitter ranking Clamp output to [0,1]; root-cause fixture replay
CLI subcommand panic on malformed CSV Process exit non-zero Single query failure; no budget charge Structured error JSON; budget unchanged

13.10 Operational Notes

Topic Detail
Logging tracing JSON; fields: mechanism, epsilon, delta, remaining_budget; NEVER log raw data
Metrics Expose: budget_spent_epsilon, budget_spent_delta, queries_denied_total as prometheus-style counters
Budget ledger Inspect with dp-cli budget --status; human-readable JSON snapshot
DpSgd audit Each training run emits final (eps, delta) to stdout; record in model card
Rollback Restore budget.json from checkpoint; any query charged before crash is considered spent (fail-closed, I3)

Phase 14 — hardening + statistical conformance + perf + packing

14.1 Objectives

  • dp-validate: implement StatDP-style empirical DP tests (neighboring-dataset distribution hypothesis tests); sensitivity audit via gradient/query probing; accountant cross-checks.
  • Fuzz dp-core/dp-sensitivity/dp-mechanism with malformed params and bounds; sampler bias tests; floating-point regression for snapping + discrete Gaussian.
  • Performance benchmarks for all core mechanisms (meet latency ceilings); workspace packaging: Cargo.toml workspace, feature flags, no_std stubs where applicable, public API audit.

14.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

dp-validate

Component Detail
Neighboring-dataset test Generate D and D' differing by one record; draw N samples from M(D) and M(D'); run one-sided KS test (or binomial test on threshold crossings); report p-value and pass/fail at significance level alpha=0.001
StatDP-style witness search For a grid of output thresholds, compute empirical Pr[M(D) in S] / Pr[M(D') in S]; flag if ratio exceeds e^eps + eps_slack
Sensitivity audit Black-box probe: vary a single input value across [lower, upper]; compute empirical max output shift; compare to declared sensitivity.l1
Accountant cross-check Run N compositions; compare RdpAccountant to AdvancedComposition result; assert RDP <= advanced_composition + tolerance
zCDP vs RDP check Convert rho via zcdp_to_rdp; compare to direct RDP curve per order; assert within 1e-6
Sampler bias test Draw 1 M Laplace samples; fit parameters via MLE; assert `
Float-attack regression Verify snapping Laplace output granularity equals lambda (the snapping scale), not floating-point ULP

14.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Detection

Sensitivity audit misses corner values Audit passes but privacy broken at boundary Probing only interior of [lower, upper] Force probe at exactly lower, upper, and lower + ULP
Accountant cross-check wrong direction Assert RDP >= adv-comp instead of <= Inverted inequality Unit test with known analytic values
Criterion unstable on CI Benchmark flaps above ceiling CI machine contention Use --sample-size 10 + warm-up; gate on relative not absolute
cargo fuzz ASAN stack-overflow False positive panic Deep recursion in parser Increase ASAN_OPTIONS=stack-size; flatten recursive parsers

14.7 Performance Concerns

Concern Mitigation
Snapping sampler uniform draw Use getrandom via CSPRNG; avoid per-sample syscall with buffered entropy pool
Discrete Gaussian rejection loop Expected iterations O(1); profile tail at extreme sigma to catch runaway loops
RDP accountant O(orders * steps) Pre-allocate fixed-size order vector; avoid heap alloc per step
Criterion compile time Feature-gate benchmarks behind bench feature; exclude from default build
Budget ledger contention In-process: RwLock<Budget>; ledger file write only on explicit flush or session end
Large histogram parallel composition Bins processed in parallel via rayon; per-bin noise draw independent

14.8 Edge Cases

Case Handling
Mechanism output is NaN or Inf Reject in DpResult construction; return SamplerError::InvalidOutput
Neighboring datasets identical KS test trivially passes; log warning — test is vacuous
Sensitivity audit with lower == upper Single-valued domain; declared sensitivity must be 0; assert
cargo fuzz input exactly 0 bytes Handle gracefully in all parsers; no panic
Cross-check with zero compositions Accountant reports eps=0; assert matches
Benchmark on machines with no RDTSC criterion falls back to wall-clock; results valid
delta = 1 - 1e-15 near boundary Accepted if < 1; Gaussian sigma formula numerically stable

Benchmark regression above ceiling CI gate fails Block release until resolved Profile with perf; optimize critical path
no_std build breaks CI cross-compile job fails Embedded use-case broken Audit feature gates; restore no_std compatibility

Threat Model

Trust Boundaries

TRUST BOUNDARY MAP
  [Analyst / CLI caller]  ← untrusted: epsilon, delta, bounds, query params, data
        |
        v  validated at dp-core newtypes; Bounds checked at dp-sensitivity
  [dp-query / dp-budget]  ← semi-trusted once initialized; budget ceiling set by operator
        |
        v  sensitivity computed internally (trusted); Bounds clamping enforced
  [dp-mechanism / dp-sample]  ← trusted compute path; CSPRNG is OS-level (trusted)
        |
        v  every release charges accountant before emitting DpResult
  [dp-accountant]  ← trusted; arithmetic only, no external I/O
        |
        v  budget gate is the last hard barrier
  [dp-budget]  ← trusted; atomic ledger; append-only audit log
        |
        v
  [DpResult<T>]  ← post-processing immune; safe to return to untrusted caller

Threat actors:

  • Malicious analyst: crafts Bounds, epsilon, or query structure to extract raw data or exceed budget.
  • Buggy caller: passes wrong bounds, reuses a query result as input, transposes epsilon and delta.
  • Supply-chain adversary: compromises sampler crate, CSPRNG, or a dependency.
  • Operator mistake: misconfigures total budget ceiling, resets ledger without recording, deploys wrong binary version.
  • Resource exhaustion attacker: submits many expensive queries to exhaust compute or budget.
  • Reconstruction attacker: combines multiple DP releases to narrow confidence intervals below the DP guarantee (mitigated by composition accounting and hard budget ceiling — I3, I4).
  • Membership inference adversary: uses released statistics to determine whether a specific individual was in the dataset (mitigated by the DP guarantee itself — I1; the engine's job is to ensure the guarantee holds, not to prevent inference within the proven bound).

Threat Table

ID Threat Surface Attack Vector Mitigation Detection Telemetry Recovery
T1 Sensitivity underflow — declared bounds tighter than actual data range; noise too small Bounds param to dp-sensitivity Analyst supplies false [lower,upper]; clamping truncates silently Enforce clamping before aggregation (I6); dp-validate sensitivity audit; declared >= empirical (I2) Sensitivity audit in dp-validate; noised output suspiciously close to exact sens_declared vs sens_empirical log field Re-run with corrected Bounds; mark prior releases suspect
T2 Budget probing — binary-search on budget denial threshold to infer remaining epsilon dp-budget spend API Submit queries near boundary; observe deny/allow Fail-closed (I10); do not expose remaining budget in error body; rate-limit deny responses Spike in dp_budget_deny_total; repeated near-boundary queries dp_budget_deny_total counter per analyst_id Suspend analyst token; rotate dataset_id
T3 Floating-point channel — naive float Laplace/Gaussian reveals exact input through representable-value gaps dp-sample Observe many samples; infer input from LSB distribution Snapping mechanism (Mironov CCS 2012) for Laplace; discrete Gaussian (Canonne NeurIPS 2020) for Gaussian (I5, P3) LSB uniformity test in dp-validate; dp-validate Phase 14 statistical conformance sampler_type label on every release span Patch dp-sample; re-audit all releases using affected sampler
T4 Epsilon/delta type confusion — caller swaps epsilon and delta values dp-core public API No strong typing before newtypes at call site Epsilon(f64) and Delta(f64) newtypes enforced at construction; no implicit numeric coercion Epsilon(0.3) accepted; Delta(2.0) rejected at construction; property test dp_param_invalid_total{param="delta"} Fix at call site; types enforce at runtime
T5 Unbounded sensitivity — analyst queries unclamped data; sensitivity = ∞; noise scale = ∞ dp-sensitivity Missing or infinite Bounds → L1/L2 = f64::INFINITY Reject unbounded queries; require explicit Bounds for all real-valued queries (P7, I10) Err::UnboundedSensitivity in logs dp_sensitivity_unbounded_total counter Caller must supply valid Bounds; engine never defaults to ∞
T6 Budget exhaustion DoS — adversary submits many high-epsilon queries to drain shared budget dp-budget Legitimate but aggressive queries Per-analyst sub-ledger with independent ceiling; per-analyst rate-limit; query planner warns at 80% Budget draw-down rate exceeds threshold dp_budget_spent_pct gauge; draw-down rate metric Suspend analyst token; budget reset requires two-step operator confirmation (RB-01)
T7 Accountant undercount — composition arithmetic yields epsilon < true loss dp-accountant Float accumulation across many small charges; wrong RDP formula Kahan summation for epsilon accumulation; RDP per-order addition (no cancellation); property test: composed >= individual Cross-check RDP → (eps,delta) vs advanced composition for identical sequence; invariant I4 dp_accountant_consistency_error counter Recompute with conservative basic composition; re-audit affected sequence
T8 CSPRNG weakness — low-entropy source makes noise predictable dp-sample Early-boot seeding; test code that imports SmallRng accidentally Use getrandom/OsRng exclusively; CI lint forbids rand::random or SmallRng in dp-sample Static analysis (cargo-clippy custom lint); CI deny list — Patch; re-issue releases; rotate dataset
T9 Supply-chain compromise of noise / RNG crate dp-sample dependencies Malicious patch to rand_core, getrandom, or other dep Cargo.lock pinning; cargo-audit and cargo-deny in CI; reproducible builds; optional vendoring cargo-audit failure in CI build_audit_result label Pin to patched version; rebuild; re-run full sampler validation suite
T10 Privacy ledger corruption — concurrent writes produce double-spend or lost spends dp-budget Race condition if budget store accessed from multiple threads/processes Atomic CAS on spent value; Mutex-protected or transactional KV store; no async mid-spend path Ledger monotonicity invariant check on load; spent never decreases dp_ledger_write_conflict_total Replay append-only audit log to reconstruct true spent; reject inconsistent snapshot
T11 DP-SGD gradient accumulation attack — many gradient releases enable training-data reconstruction dp-dpsgd Attacker receives per-step DpResult; accumulates across epochs Moments accountant tracks cumulative (eps,delta) per training run (Phase 10); total budget enforced via dp-budget; early stop if ceiling approached dp_dpsgd_epsilon_per_epoch gauge; alert if cumulative epsilon > ceiling dpsgd_total_epsilon gauge per run_id Stop training early (RB-05); log final (eps,delta); do not resume without new budget
T12 Operator resets budget inadvertently — wipes ledger without audit dp-budget / dp-cli Privileged CLI operator error Two-step confirmation for reset; append RESET event to audit log before clearing; audit log survives reset budget_reset event in audit log; alert on unexpected reset Audit log entry Replay audit log; alert data steward; review whether privacy guarantees were honored
T13 Histogram bin smuggling — analyst controls bin boundaries to isolate individual records dp-query histogram Narrow bins each containing one person; sensitivity still claimed = 1 Parallel composition valid only when bins are disjoint and committed pre-query; bin structure frozen after data ingestion (I6) Validate bin disjointness and coverage before sensitivity computation — Reject dynamic bin redefinition post-data-ingestion; require pre-committed bin schema
T14 Exponential mechanism candidate-set DoS — large candidate set causes CPU exhaustion dp-mechanism ExponentialMechanism Caller passes exponentially large candidate vector Cap candidate set size at construction; O(n) softmax is linear but n must be bounded; return Err if candidates > configured max CPU time metric per mechanism call; timeout alert
T15 Sensitivity inflation — analyst inflates claimed sensitivity to draw smaller noise than warranted dp-sensitivity sensitivity computation Caller passes Sensitivity directly instead of computing from data/bounds dp-sensitivity always computes sensitivity from Bounds, not from caller-supplied Sensitivity; Sensitivity is a result, not an input Property test: sensitivity(clamp(data,B)) >= empirical sensitivity — API design: sensitivity is computed by dp-sensitivity, not accepted from caller
T16 Replay of old DpResult as fresh — attacker re-presents a prior release to appear as a new query dp-budget / DpResult Attacker reuses a timestamped DpResult DpResult carries no budget deduction on replay; budget is charged per-query, not per-result; idempotency not required since results are one-way Audit log correlates query_id with release; duplicate query_id flagged query_id in every audit log entry Reject duplicate query_id if replay is a concern; mark query_id used

Failure Modes and Recovery

Class Symptoms Root Causes Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery Operator Actions Forensic Artifacts Long-Term Mitigation
F1: Invalid PrivacyParams Err on construction; no release emitted eps=0, NaN, ∞; delta>=1 or <0 Newtype constructor Err; property test I8 Propagate Err; no partial release Fix caller params Audit call site Error log with raw param values Newtype enforcement in dp-core Phase 1
F2: Sensitivity overflow Err::UnboundedSensitivity; query refused Unclamped data; Bounds not supplied; arithmetic overflow in sensitivity dp-sensitivity Err path; dp_sensitivity_unbounded_total counter Deny query (I10) Supply explicit Bounds covering data domain Verify Bounds match data domain; update data contract Query params in error log Mandatory Bounds for all real-valued queries (P7); API enforces
F3: Budget exhausted BudgetExceeded Err; no value returned Accumulated spend reached total ceiling dp-budget spend() returns Err::Insufficient Deny release; ledger unchanged Provision new dataset_id with fresh budget; or request budget expansion Audit spend log; decide whether to expand; data steward approval Audit log of all prior charges; final spent value Per-analyst sub-ledgers; pre-query budget check with warning at 80% (RB-01)
F4: Accountant divergence Composed (eps,delta) under-reports true loss; privacy violation possible Float accumulation; wrong RDP-to-(eps,delta) conversion; missing charge Cross-check RDP vs advanced composition in Phase 14; invariant I4 test None — correctness failure Patch accountant; recalculate true spend for affected sequence Mark affected releases suspect; notify data steward Accountant state snapshot; mechanism sequence log Kahan summation; cross-validation tests in dp-validate; Phase 14
F5: Sampler distribution failure dp-validate neighboring-dataset test fails; LSB distribution non-uniform Bug in snapping/discrete Gaussian; CSPRNG failure or substitution dp-validate test suite; dp_validate_test_pass{test="lsb_uniform"} = 0 None — correctness failure; no auto fix Patch dp-sample; re-run full Phase 14 validation before re-enabling Quarantine affected mechanism; halt releases Failed test report; sampler iteration log; CSPRNG entropy source Rigorous Phase 5 implementation; Phase 14 statistical conformance; CI gate
F6: Budget ledger corruption spent > total without denial; or spent decreases after queries; ledger replay diverges Race condition; storage corruption; improper reset without audit log entry Monotonicity invariant on every ledger load; audit log replay vs snapshot Reconstruct ledger from append-only audit log Replay audit log; reject inconsistent snapshot; lock ledger during recovery Lock dataset_id; replay; notify analyst; determine if over-budget releases occurred Audit log file; pre-corruption snapshot; dp_ledger_write_conflict_total Atomic CAS; Mutex; append-only audit log; Phase 11
F7: Composition soundness failure Cumulative reported (eps,delta) smaller than true composed privacy loss Wrong per-mechanism RDP formula; missing charge for a release Property test: compose N identical Gaussian mechanisms, compare to closed-form sigma^{-2} * N * alpha None Patch accountant formula; re-audit affected sequence Recalculate true spend; mark over-budget; inform data steward Mechanism type + params for each charged release Unit test per mechanism RDP formula vs analytic; Phase 9
F8: DP-SGD budget blowout Training stops mid-epoch with BudgetExceeded; partial model Under-estimated per-step epsilon; more steps than planned; sigma too small Moments accountant check before each gradient step; dp_dpsgd_epsilon_per_epoch alert Stop training; return partial model with logged final (eps,delta) Resume with new budget allocation or re-tune sigma/clip_norm Evaluate model utility vs privacy; decide whether to publish partial model Per-step epsilon log; final (eps,delta) at stop; clip_norm histogram Pre-training budget projection tool; Phase 13 budget planner
F9: CLI mechanism mismatch Wrong mechanism applied to query type; sensitivity mismatch Operator selects mechanism manually, bypasses dp-query sensitivity wiring dp-cli validates mechanism-query type compatibility at parse time Return Err::IncompatibleMechanism Re-run with correct subcommand Document intended mechanism per query type in runbooks CLI invocation log with mechanism and query type dp-query enforces mechanism selection; CLI rejects incompatible combinations
F10: Discrete Gaussian rejection loop Sampler CPU spike; no sample returned; timeout Rejection-sampling tail probability too low; sigma miscalibrated (too small relative to sensitivity) Sampler iteration count > max_iterations threshold; dp_sampler_iterations_p99 alert Return Err::SamplerTimeout after max iterations; no partial sample Debug sigma calibration; verify l2 sensitivity / epsilon / delta inputs Check sigma relative to sensitivity; verify Balle-Wang calibration Sampler iteration count histogram; sigma value Proven rejection bound from Canonne et al; hard iteration cap with Err return; Phase 5
F11: Exponential mechanism numeric underflow All candidates receive probability ≈ 0; output always same candidate; entropy test fails Utility values have extreme range; exp(utility) underflows to 0.0 Output entropy near zero; unit test with extreme utility range None Rescale utilities; use log-sum-exp Review utility function range; document expected range in API Utility value distribution log Numerically stable log-sum-exp in Phase 6; document utility range contract
F12: Post-processing misuse Analyst re-noises a DpResult, degrading utility unnecessarily (no privacy violation — I7) Misunderstanding of post-processing closure Code review; dp-validate warns if DpResult is passed as input to a mechanism Warn (not an error; I7 guarantees DP preserved) Educate analyst; redesign pipeline Document P5 in operator runbook — P5 in design principles documentation; CLI warning message
F13: Delta=0 passed to Gaussian Err::DeltaRequiredForGaussian; no release Caller uses pure-DP params with Gaussian mechanism GaussianMechanism constructor checks delta > 0 (I8) Propagate Err Switch to Laplace mechanism for pure DP; or supply delta > 0 Document mechanism-parameter compatibility Error log API documentation; Phase 4 rejects delta=0 explicitly
F14: Audit log disk full Audit log write fails; budget charge cannot be confirmed Disk exhaustion on log volume Log write Err propagates; budget charge aborted (fail-closed: charge not recorded = charge not accepted) Deny query if log write fails (fail-closed) Free disk space; resume; no charge is lost because denied Monitor disk on log volume; alert at 80% usage Disk usage metric; last successful log entry timestamp Log rotation and retention policy; log to separate volume from data
F15: Snapping mechanism precision loss Released values cluster on a coarse grid; output unusually quantized Scale parameter b too large relative to float precision; incorrect rounding in snapping Sampler histogram in dp-validate has suspicious gaps None — noise is still correct but coarser; not a privacy failure Adjust query type or sensitivity; use discrete Gaussian Review sensitivity calibration; document expected output precision Sampler output histogram; noise scale log Document snapping granularity bounds per sensitivity range; Phase 5
F16: RDP order vector misconfiguration Tightest (eps,delta) conversion misses the optimal order; budget charged too conservatively RDP orders vector too coarse; missing small or large orders (eps,delta) conversion cross-check shows gap > threshold Use a default fine-grained order vector [1.01, 1.1, ..., 512] Re-tune order vector; re-run accountant Document recommended order vector in RB-04 variant Accountant state with orders; converted (eps,delta) log Default fine-grained order vector shipped with RdpAccountant; user override documented
F17: zCDP to (eps,delta) conversion mismatch zCDP-derived (eps,delta) differs significantly from RDP-derived for same mechanism Using the wrong conversion formula or wrong delta target Invariant test: zCDP rho → (eps,delta) vs RDP for Gaussian mechanism None Identify which conversion is being used; patch formula Document canonical conversion: rho → eps = rho + 2sqrt(rholn(1/delta)) Accountant type label in every release span Phase 10 cross-checks zCDP vs RDP conversion; property test

Operational Readiness

Metrics Strategy

Metric Type Labels Alert Threshold
dp_budget_spent_pct Gauge dataset_id, analyst_id > 80% warn; > 95% page
dp_budget_deny_total Counter dataset_id, mechanism, analyst_id Rate > 10/min: alert (probing)
dp_query_latency_ns Histogram (P50/P99/P999) query_type, mechanism P99 > 10 ms alert
dp_mechanism_noise_scale Histogram mechanism, query_type Out of expected range: warn
dp_accountant_epsilon_total Gauge dataset_id, accountant_type Mirror of budget_spent; alert if diverges
dp_sampler_iterations_p99 Histogram sampler_type P99 > 500 warn; > 1000 page
dp_param_invalid_total Counter param, reason, crate Any spike: alert
dp_ledger_write_conflict_total Counter dataset_id > 0: page immediately
dp_dpsgd_epsilon_per_epoch Gauge run_id > budget/N_epochs: warn
dp_dpsgd_epsilon_total Gauge run_id > ceiling: page
dp_validate_test_pass Gauge (0/1) mechanism, test_type = 0: page immediately
dp_sensitivity_unbounded_total Counter query_type Any: alert
dp_budget_reset_total Counter dataset_id, operator_id Any unplanned: alert
dp_audit_log_write_errors_total Counter dataset_id > 0: page (fail-closed path)

All metrics exported via Prometheus text format; Grafana dashboards consume them. Histograms use exponential buckets. Labels are low-cardinality (dataset_id is a hash, not raw name). Metrics are emitted per-call; no aggregation inside the engine — aggregation happens at the metrics collector.

Per-Crate Health Signals

Crate Key Health Signal Healthy Value
dp-core dp_param_invalid_total rate Near zero in steady state
dp-sensitivity dp_sensitivity_unbounded_total Zero
dp-sample dp_sampler_iterations_p99 < 100 (Discrete Gaussian); = 1 (Laplace snapping)
dp-mechanism dp_mechanism_noise_scale distribution Stable across deploys for same query type
dp-accountant dp_accountant_epsilon_total monotone Never decreases within a session
dp-budget dp_budget_spent_pct Steady increase; no sudden jumps
dp-budget dp_budget_deny_total rate Low; spike = attack or misconfiguration
dp-query dp_query_latency_ns P99 < 10 ms
dp-validate dp_validate_test_pass Always 1 in prod
dp-dpsgd dp_dpsgd_epsilon_total Monotone; stays below ceiling

Tracing Strategy

  • Every DpResult<T> emission opens a root span: fields = dataset_id, query_id, query_type, mechanism, epsilon_charged, delta_charged, sensitivity_l1, sensitivity_l2, bounds_lower, bounds_upper.
  • Nested child spans: dp-sensitivity (l1/l2 computed), dp-mechanism (noise scale, mechanism variant), dp-sample (sampler type, iteration count), dp-accountant (rdp_orders, composed_eps), dp-budget (spent_before, charge, spent_after, denied).
  • All spans export via OpenTelemetry OTLP; structured as JSON for SIEM ingestion.
  • Never record in any span: raw data values, un-noised query answers, individual input records (P10).
  • Trace sampling: 100% for budget-denial and validation-failure events; configurable rate (default 1%) for normal releases.

Structured Logging

Field Type Present When
ts ISO-8601 Always
level ERROR/WARN/INFO/DEBUG Always
crate string Always
event string enum Always
dataset_id string (hash) Always
analyst_id string Always
query_id UUID Per query
mechanism string Per mechanism invocation
epsilon_charged f64 Per release
delta_charged f64 Per release
sensitivity_l1 f64 Per sensitivity computation
sensitivity_l2 f64 Per sensitivity computation
denied bool Per budget check
error string On Err
  • Levels: ERROR (param invalid, budget denial, invariant violation, audit log write failure), WARN (budget > 80%, sampler slow, utility underflow), INFO (every DpResult emission with (eps,delta)), DEBUG (RDP intermediate values per order — disabled in production).
  • Audit log: separate append-only file; JSON lines; one entry per budget charge event; SHA-256 chained integrity; schema_version field; rotated daily; retained 90 days minimum; encrypted at rest.
  • Never log: raw data, clamped per-record values, un-noised query answers (P10, I10).
